These results reveal a pronounced gap between reasoning over curated evidence and acquiring that evidence directly from WSIs. PathAgentBench provides a unified framework for measuring and improving evidence-seeking pathology models.

</details>


### [140] [No Training, Better Flights: Test-Time Scaled VLMs for UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19288)
*Feinan Cheng,Dongliang Xu,Wenli Nong,Zhiheng Zhang,Ang Liu,Tianyu Wang,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外训练的测试时扩展方法，通过迭代精炼、多候选生成、自纠正和多标准评分，显著提升了无人机视觉语言导航的性能，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉语言导航方法通常依赖单次推理，在复杂环境中容易产生次优或不安全的轨迹，缺乏自我修正能力。

Method: 采用测试时扩展策略：首先生成多个平行候选导航计划，然后执行自纠正步骤让模型重新评估初始计划，最后设计包含安全性、目标对齐和前进进度维度的多标准评分函数对精炼候选进行评价。

Result: 在无人机视觉语言导航任务上取得了SOTA性能，冻结模型无需重新训练即可实现更准确和可靠的飞行规划。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展是一种简单而有效的无需训练的方法，能够显著增强VLMs在导航任务中的推理鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling offers a promising method to improve the inference performance of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) without additional training. Existing approaches to vision-language navigation (VLN) for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) typically relies on a single inference pass, which can falter in complex environments by producing suboptimal or unsafe trajectories. In this paper, we explore a simple and effective approach to apply test-time scaling to VLN for UAV. We enhance navigation reasoning through an iterative refinement process that requires no extra model training, guiding the model to re-evaluate its initial navigation plan for better accuracy and safety. Our method first prompts the model to generate multiple parallel candidates and then performs a self-correction step, achieving deeper and more robust planning without changing the underlying model. To further strengthen decision-making, we design a multi-criteria scoring function to evaluate the refined candidates based on safety, goal alignment, and forward-progress. This simple yet powerful combination enables a frozen UAV navigation VLMs to self-correct and generate more accurate and reliable flight plans, achieving SOTA performance in this task.

</details>


### [141] [ERank in Latent Space as an Image-Complexity and Richness Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19315)
*Maksim Smirnov,Grigory Kononov,Anastasiia Linich,Egor Surkov,Egor Shvetsov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出有效秩（ERank）作为图像视觉丰富度的无标签度量，基于预训练编码器单次前向传播计算特征图通道协方差的有效秩，实验表明其与复杂度标注、码率等指标相关，并可作为数据选择标准提升超分辨率和OCR任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像复杂度度量缺乏既无需标签又计算高效的指标，且难以直接服务于下游任务的数据选择。

Method: 计算冻结预训练编码器提取的深度特征图通道协方差矩阵的有效秩（ERank），统计图像激活的去相关通道方向数量，并分析其在噪声下的行为特性。

Result: ERank与IC9600人类复杂度标注相关系数r=0.72，与码率、清晰度、边缘密度相关；低ERank样本去除提升超分辨率，高ERank样本去除提升OCR，但对分类、分割、去噪无帮助。

Conclusion: ERank是一种廉价且有价值的视觉丰富度信号，在任务难度由输入丰富度主导的场景中尤为有效。

Abstract: We propose the effective rank (ERank) of the channel covariance of an image's deep feature map as a per-sample, label-free measure of visual richness, computed from a single forward pass through a frozen pretrained encoder. ERank counts how many decorrelated channel directions an image activates, and we characterize its properties, including its behavior under noise. Empirically, ERank orders images from plain to visually rich, correlates with codec bitrate, sharpness, and edge density, and correlates with human complexity annotations on IC9600 with $r = 0.72$. As a data-selection criterion, removing low-ERank samples improves super-resolution and removing high-ERank samples improves OCR, in both pretraining and finetuning, while selection does not help classification, segmentation, or denoising. ERank is thus a cheap richness signal, useful exactly when task difficulty is governed by input richness.

</details>


### [142] [InstructMixup: Instruction-Guided Salient Patch Editing for Robust Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19324)
*Khawar Islam,Arif Mahmood,Xin Jin,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为InstructMixup的数据增强方法，通过在单样本内提取显著区域、用指令引导生成模型编辑并混合分形结构，构建具有挑战性但标签一致的训练样本，在多基准测试中优于九种主流增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合类数据增强方法存在计算开销大（计算信息性混合区域）以及跨样本混合破坏语义完整性的问题，亟需一种既能生成困难样本又能保持标签一致性的高效数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 使用轻量级显著性检测器提取多尺度显著区域；2) 通过指令引导生成模型编辑显著区域，并离线缓存以降低训练开销；3) 将编辑后的区域融合回原样本的非显著区域；4) 在相同显著区域中按自适应比例注入自相似分形结构，使每个样本同时包含分形与非分形特征；5) 推导了邻域风险的二阶近似来理论分析方法的有效性。

Result: 在CNN、ViT、视觉-语言基础模型上，跨越7个基准（粗/细粒度分类、遮挡/污染鲁棒性、校准、迁移学习、自监督学习），InstructMixup全面优于9种对比增强方法，在所有基准上均超越最强基线。

Conclusion: InstructMixup通过在单样本内进行生成式编辑与分形结构注入，实现了高效、标签一致且具有挑战性的数据增强，理论分析验证了该方法能增强对生成编辑的不变性并抑制显著方向上的曲率，是一种通用且强大的数据增强新范式。

Abstract: In image and video technologies, data augmentation is widely used to improve the generalization of deep visual models, and mixup-based strategies that interpolate between samples have become the dominant approach. However, computing informative mixing regions adds substantial overhead, and blending content across different images frequently disrupts the semantic integrity of the resulting sample. We propose \our{}, a data augmentation method that constructs challenging yet label-consistent training samples entirely within a single visual sample. \our{} first extracts multi-scale salient patches from the sample using a lightweight saliency detector, refines each patch with an instruction-guided generative model, and blends the edited patch back into the non-salient regions of the same sample; because the generative edits are computed once and cached offline, this step adds negligible training cost. To further diversify the learned representation, \our{} injects self-similar fractal structure into the same salient regions at an adaptive ratio, so each training sample carries both fractal and non-fractal structure. We derive a second-order approximation of the resulting vicinal risk, showing that the method simultaneously enforces invariance to the generative edit and suppresses curvature along the perturbed salient directions, and we verify both predictions empirically. We evaluate on small to large backbones for instance Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs) and Vision-Language Foundational Models (VLMs) across seven benchmarks covering coarse- and fine-grained classification, robustness to corruption and occlusion, calibration, and transfer and self-supervised learning, InstructMixup outperforms nine competing augmentation methods, surpassing the strongest baseline across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [143] [OmniReasoner: Thinking with Long Audio-Video via Native Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19339)
*Yu Chen,Caorui Li,Ziyu Xiong,Yidong Wang,Mingqi Gao,Shuman Liu,Biao Liu,Chunfeng Yang,Anxiang Zeng,Haibo Zhang,Chaofan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniReasoner是一个面向长音频-视频推理的思维训练框架，通过工具调用（zoom-in）策略，让全模态大语言模型学会在低代价全局预览后，针对关键时空区域进行高保真局部检查，从而提升推理准确性和时间定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音频-视频推理中，关键证据稀疏、跨模态，且保持全序列统一高保真度的计算成本过高，现有全模态大语言模型难以有效处理。

Method: 提出OmniReasoner框架：1) 工具调用后训练，通过SFT+RL让模型学会在必要时调用zoom-in工具聚焦关键时空片段；2) TimeAnchor机制保持工具参数在不同采样粒度间的语义一致性；3) 时序增强数据引擎，通过视频编辑合成工具调用的训练轨迹，避免昂贵的人工区间标注。

Result: 在全模态和视频基准测试上，OmniReasoner同时提升了答案准确性和时间定位能力，并将高保真计算集中在信息丰富的区域。

Conclusion: 通过让模型自主学习何时、何处调用zoom-in工具进行局部高保真检查，可以在不增加全序列计算负担的前提下，有效提升长音频-视频推理性能。

Abstract: Long audio-video reasoning is difficult for omnimodal LLMs because the decisive evidence is often sparse, cross-modal, and too expensive to preserve with uniformly high-fidelity inputs. We introduce OmniReasoner, a tool-use post-training framework for Thinking with Long Audio-Video: omni-modal LLMs learn, via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, to decide whether and where to call a zoom-in tool before answering. OmniReasoner first builds a low-cost global preview of the full stream and then, when needed, calls the zoom-in tool with a requested temporal interval for higher-fidelity visual and audio inspection before answering. Because the model observes different sampling granularities before and after this call -- a sparse global preview and a denser local clip -- we introduce TimeAnchor, which keeps the tool's temporal argument valid and round-trip-consistent across these granularities, rather than tied to frame indices from a particular sampling rate. To make this tool-use behavior trainable without expensive manual interval annotation, we build a Temporal Augmented Data Engine that synthesizes tool-use post-training trajectories by video editing and composition. Experiments across omnimodal and video benchmarks show that OmniReasoner improves both answer accuracy and temporal grounding while concentrating high-fidelity computation on informative regions. Code is available at https://github.com/RockyChen0205/OmniReasoner.

</details>


### [144] [ExpertVerse: A General-Purpose Benchmark for Expert-Level Reasoning in Knowledge-Intensive Visual Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19341)
*Yuan Wang,Yongchao Du,Mengting Chen,Jinsong Lan,Xuetao Feng,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExpertVerse是一个包含9种认知能力和58个细分学科的知识密集型视觉生成基准，用于评估生成模型在知识推理方面的缺陷。同时提出KnowThinker模型和BPPO优化方法，以提升跨模态推理和生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽能进行指令式图像生成，但仅限于显式常识推理、浅层因果理解和直接知识回忆，无法处理知识密集型的生成任务。因此亟需一个知识密集型基准来系统评估生成模型的高阶认知推理能力。

Method: 构建ExpertVerse基准，包含9种认知能力×8个专家学科的58个细分方向，共1,611个专家标注实例。开发自动化流程生成ExpertVerse-100K大规模数据集。提出KnowThinker模型，采用RL微调并结合BPPO（含Bootstrapping Reward Rectification和Conflict-Aware Pareto Advantage Fusion）解决多奖励优化中的跨模态信用分配偏差和多目标梯度冲突问题。

Result: 实验表明，无论开源模型还是闭源模型均暴露出显著的推理缺陷，验证了知识密集型基准对于推动下一代视觉生成的重要性。

Conclusion: 现有生成模型在知识密集型推理任务上存在严重不足，亟需像ExpertVerse这样的基准来引导未来研究；提出的KnowThinker和BPPO方法为跨模态知识推理生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal generative models have enabled instruction-based image generation to move beyond semantic manipulation to knowledge-driven visual reasoning. However, these methods focus on explicit commonsense reasoning, shallow causal understanding, and direct knowledge recall, failing at knowledge-intensive generation. We develop \textbf{ExpertVerse}, a capability-centric benchmark to evaluate generative models via knowledge-intensive lens. ExpertVerse stratifies reasoning generation across an orthogonal taxonomy of \textit{9 cognitive capabilities} and \textit{8 expert disciplines}, yielding \textit{58 sub-disciplines}. We curate 1,611 expert-annotated instances covering single-image editing, multi-image composition, and text-to-image generation. We further develop an automated workflow to produce \textbf{ExpertVerse-100K}, a large-scale dataset with reasoning traces and knowledge-anchored rationale annotations. Based on this, we train \textbf{KnowThinker} with RL fine-tuning, a VLM reasoning engine with world knowledge that jointly generates thinking processes and refined instructions. Towards the cross-modal credit misalignment and multi-objective gradient conflicts in multi-reward optimization, we propose a tailored Bootstrapped Pareto Policy Optimization (BPPO), which synergizes Bootstrapping Reward Rectification (BRR) and Conflict-Aware Pareto Advantage Fusion (CPAF). Extensive results of both open-source and proprietary models exposes critical reasoning deficits, highlighting imperative for knowledge-intensive benchmarks towards next-generation visual generation.

</details>


### [145] [Masked Visual Actions for Unified World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19343)
*Hadi Alzayer,Wenlong Huang,Haonan Chen,Christopher Luey,Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala,Gordon Wetzstein,Li Fei-Fei,Yilun Du,Jiajun Wu,Jia-Bin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Masked Visual Actions (MVA)，一种像素空间控制接口，通过在视频中部分揭示任意实体的轨迹来表达动作。仅需15小时掩码示例微调，单一模型即可实现前向动力学预测、逆向建模和模型规划，在多种场景和实体上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型蕴含丰富的视觉世界交互先验，但如何将动作信息以与视觉空间对齐的形式传达给这些模型，使其既能利用学习到的交互先验又能扎根于物理操作，是核心挑战。

Method: 提出Masked Visual Actions (MVA)，将动作表示为视频中任意实体的部分被揭示轨迹。揭示机器人运动使模型充当前向动力学模型（预测场景对low-level动作的响应），揭示期望物体运动使模型逆向恢复机器人行为。使用真实视频和仿真中仅15小时的掩码示例进行微调。

Result: 单个模型在多种场景和实体上实现强视觉保真度和可控性。在操作任务中，模型生成的想象 rollout 与实际执行结果相关，可用于策略评估；通过排序候选未来状态改进模型规划中的决策；支持从期望物体运动合成机器人运动的逆向建模。

Conclusion: MVA提供了一种有效的像素空间控制接口，以极少的微调数据使视频模型具备机器人世界建模能力，支持前向预测、规划评估和逆向建模等多种下游应用。

Abstract: Video models absorb rich priors over how the visual world moves, interacts, and responds to contact, making them promising substrates for robotic world modeling. The central challenge is how to communicate action to such models in a form aligned with the visual space in which they learned these interaction priors, yet still grounded in physical manipulation. We introduce Masked Visual Actions, a pixel-space control interface that expresses action as a partially revealed trajectory of an arbitrary entity in a video. Revealing robot motion makes the model act as a forward dynamics model that predicts the scene's response to low-level robot actions, while revealing desired object motion makes the same model recover robot behavior consistent with that outcome. Finetuned with only 15 hours of masked examples from real videos and simulation, a single checkpoint achieves strong visual fidelity and controllability across diverse scenes and multiple embodiments. In downstream manipulation settings, the model produces imagined rollouts whose outcomes correlate with real-world execution for policy evaluation, improves decision making by ranking candidate futures in model-based planning, and supports inverse modeling by synthesizing robot motion from desired object motion.

</details>


### [146] [Appearance Pointers -- Multimodal Region Control of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19344)
*Rahul Sajnani,Yulia Gryaditskaya,Radomír Měch,Srinath Sridhar,Matheus Gadelha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出外观指针（Appearance Pointers），一种无需重新训练基础模型即可为扩散Transformer（DiT）提供区域级多模态控制的方法，通过区域对应网络和空间聚合机制，在多种指标上达到或超越现有模态特定方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意 professionals 需要精确的区域级控制（如材质、物体身份、空间布局），但现有的文本提示方法难以可靠实现，而DiT虽能原生处理文本和图像混合token，却缺乏决定token应影响输出位置和方式的机制。

Method: 提出外观指针——紧凑的引导token，通过区域对应网络生成，并经过空间聚合机制精炼，将文本或图像输入与用户指定的掩码对齐，引导DiT在正确的空间位置获取正确的外观线索，支持多个区域描述而不显著增加token负载。

Result: 单个模型在多种指标上达到或超越了模态特定的最新方法的表现。

Conclusion: 这是首个无需重新训练基础模型即可为DiT提供模态无关的局部多模态控制接口的方法，为生成式图像合成中精确、区域感知的多模态引导提供了一条简单且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Controllable image generation remains challenging for creative professionals, who often require precise regional control over materials, object identities, and spatial arrangements that cannot be reliably achieved through text prompting alone. Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can natively ingest heterogeneous tokens stemming from texts and images, but they lack mechanisms for determining where and how these tokens should influence the output. We introduce appearance pointers, compact tokens that guide DiTs toward the correct appearance cues at the correct spatial locations by aligning text or image inputs with user-specified masks. Appearance pointers are produced by a region correspondence network and refined through a spatial aggregation mechanism, enabling the model to handle multiple regional descriptions without significantly increasing token load. Our approach introduces the first modality-agnostic interface for localized multimodal control in a DiT without retraining the base model from scratch. Across a range of metrics, our single model reaches or surpasses the performance of modality-specific state of the art methods, offering a simple and extensible path toward precise, region-aware, multimodal guidance in generative image synthesis.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Shared Vulnerabilities in Robustness-Optimized Defenses: One Breach Exposes the Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18339)
*Hanrui Wang,Ruihao Zheng,Shuo Wang,Isao Echizen,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对抗性鲁棒性优化（如对抗训练、对抗净化）存在一个安全风险：这些方法会在不同防御之间创建共享的脆弱性。一旦一种代表性防御被攻破，整个防御家族可能暴露。作者提出了更严格的传输独有协议和简单自适应攻击PGDTransfer来测试这一风险，并引入对抗敏感性图（AdvSM）进行可视化。实验表明净化防御面临严重风险：PGDTransfer在ε=4/255下平均传输攻击成功率达80.4%。未来防御应将脆弱性多样性和传输独有隔离作为安全目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性鲁棒性优化方法在提升个体防御性能的同时，可能在不同防御之间引入共享脆弱性，导致一旦某种代表性防御被攻破，整个防御家族面临暴露风险。这种系统性风险尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出传输独有协议（区分真正可迁移性与失真导致的性能下降和复杂攻击的算法增益）；设计简单自适应攻击方法PGDTransfer；引入对抗敏感性图（AdvSM）可视化和量化跨可微与不可微防御的对齐程度。

Result: 在对抗训练分类器、净化防御和具有鲁棒视觉编码器的大视觉语言模型上，每个鲁棒性家族内部均存在自然可迁移性。净化防御风险最为严重：PGDTransfer在ε=4/255下对滤波、压缩和扩散净化器平均传输攻击成功率达80.4%。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒性优化存在共享脆弱性的系统风险，防御设计应将脆弱性多样性和传输独有隔离作为安全目标，而非仅追求个体鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness optimization aims to preserve correct prediction under adversarial perturbations, and has produced substantial robustness gains through methods such as adversarial training and adversarial purification. However, we identify a new security risk: these gains can create shared vulnerabilities across defenses. Once one representative robustness-optimized defense is effectively breached, the broader family may become exposed. Studying this risk requires separating genuine transferability from distortion-induced degradation and from the algorithmic gains of sophisticated attacks. We therefore introduce stricter transfer-only protocols and a deliberately simple adaptive attack, PGDTransfer, to test whether robustness-optimized defenses share transfer-only vulnerability under controlled conditions. We further introduce Adversarial Sensitivity Maps (AdvSMs) to visualize and quantify shared alignment beyond differentiable classifiers, including stochastic and non-differentiable defenses. Across adversarially trained classifiers, purification-based defenses, and LVLMs with robust visual encoders, we identify natural transferability within each robustness family, i.e., transfer that arises even with simple PGD-style optimization rather than specialized transferable-attack design. The risk is already severe for purification: PGDTransfer reaches an average transfer attack success rate of $80.4\%$ across filtering-, compression-, and diffusion-based purifiers under $ε=4/255$, suggesting that purifier defenses may no longer provide reliable protection. As attacks improve, currently stronger robustness families may face the same risk. Future defenses should therefore treat vulnerability diversity and transfer-only isolation as security objectives, rather than optimizing only individual robustness.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum Cryptanalysis on IBM Quantum Hardware: Extending Even--Mansour Period Recovery from $N=4$ to $N=10$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18340)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Junghoon Shin,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在真实IBM量子硬件（Heron代，ibm\_kingston）上实现了对对称密码结构的教科书级量子密码分析，使用Simon算法恢复了Even-Mansour密码在N=10（此前最高N=4）的隐藏周期，以及3轮Feistel结构在块大小6和8的周期；还提供了涵盖四个对称密码设计范式的五种量子攻击的广度优先基准测试，并明确指出了攻击的局限性和贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在真实量子硬件上对对称密码结构的密钥恢复仅达到很小规模（如N=4），缺乏更大规模、更广泛范式的真实硬件验证，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供诚实可复现的基准测试。

Method: 在IBM Heron代量子处理器上运行Simon算法、Bernstein-Vazirani算法、Grover算法等量子攻击；针对Even-Mansour密码（N=10）、3轮Feistel结构（块大小6和8）等目标进行密钥恢复；使用错误缓解而非容错纠错；仿真验证至25量子比特。

Result: Even-Mansour密码真实硬件密钥恢复达到N=10（此前最高N=4）；3轮Feistel结构在块大小6和8上成功恢复周期；21量子比特的块大小10实例完成仿真验证并提交硬件；五种量子攻击在四种对称密码范式上完成基准测试。

Conclusion: 本文在真实量子硬件上展示了记录规模的对称密码结构量子攻击，提供了涵盖多种范式的广度基准测试，并强调这些攻击遵循生日界、不构成对经典碰撞搜索的量子优势、未破解完整AES/RSA或16轮DES，贡献在于真实硬件演示和可复现基准。

Abstract: We report genuine-un-compiled, textbook-faithful-quantum cryptanalysis of symmetric-cipher structures executed on real IBM quantum hardware (ibm\_kingston, Heron generation). Using Simon's algorithm we recover the hidden period of the Even-Mansour cipher up to security parameter N = 10 on real hardware, beyond the largest previously reported real-hardware key recovery of N = 4, and we cleanly recover the periods of a 3-round Feistel (DES-family) construction at block sizes 6 and 8; a 21-qubit block-10 instance is verified in simulation and submitted to hardware. We further provide a breadth-first benchmark of five genuine quantum attacks spanning four symmetric-cipher design paradigms -- Bernstein-Vazirani (linear structure, single query), Grover (SPN key search, quadratic), and Simon (Even-Mansour, CBC-MAC forgery, and Feistel; exponential-to-polynomial in query complexity) -- validated to the classical-simulation ceiling of 25 qubits. We are deliberately explicit about scope: these attacks target reduced or structured constructions in the Q2 (quantum-query) model, asymptotically follow the birthday bound and therefore do not constitute quantum advantage over classical collision-finding, do not break full AES/RSA or 16-round DES, and rely on error mitigation rather than fault-tolerant error correction. Our contribution is the real-hardware demonstration at record structure sizes, the breadth of genuine algorithmic coverage across four paradigms, and an honest, reproducible benchmark with public artifacts.

</details>


### [149] [PRISM: Sensitivity-Aware PolynoMial PRuning for EffIcient Neural Network Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18342)
*Sahaj Majavdia,Mahdi Taheri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地表征了结构化剪枝对同态加密神经网络可靠性的影响，并提出了可靠性感知的剪枝方法PSAP，在提升模型加密推理效率的同时大幅降低故障脆弱性，实现了效率与可靠性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝虽能降低同态加密下的神经网络推理开销，但其对模型可靠性的影响尚未被探索，现有剪枝方法缺乏可靠性意识。

Method: 提出Polynomial-Sensitivity-Aware Pruning (PSAP)，联合权重幅度、多项式激活敏感度和旋转开销三个指标对滤波器进行评分，将剪枝集中在容错区域。同时引入自适应混合度数分配方案降低乘法深度。

Result: PSAP剪枝模型将灾难性精度下降层数限制在最多2层，而基线方法为5-14层；最坏情况下脆弱性降低29倍；Halevi-Shoup旋转减少45.2%；乘法深度从66降至56层，实现了无引导的层次化推理。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持竞争性效率的同时显著提升可靠性，且故障关键层仅占参数量的1.1%，可选择性加固以最小化开销。

Abstract: Structured pruning is essential for making neural network inference feasible under homomorphic encryption (HE), yet its impact on model reliability has remained unexplored. This paper presents a systematic reliability characterization of pruned CKKS-encrypted neural networks and introduces Polynomial-Sensitivity-Aware Pruning (PSAP), a structured pruning method that is inherently reliability-aware. PSAP scores filters jointly by weight magnitude, polynomial activation sensitivity, and rotation cost, which concentrates pruning in fault-tolerant regions. Across two architectures, two datasets, two numerical representations, and five bit-error rates (40 full-model and 108 per-layer experiments), PSAP-pruned models limit catastrophic (>10 pp accuracy drop) layers to at most two versus 5--14 for magnitude-pruned baselines, reducing worst-case vulnerability by up to 29 times under int32 bit-flip injection. Direct CKKS encrypted fault injection indicates a safe operating boundary near BER~ 10^{-5}, supporting int32 injection as a conservative reliability proxy. The fault-critical structural layers account for only 1.1% of parameters, enabling selective hardening at minimal overhead. These reliability gains are obtained alongside competitive efficiency: PSAP reduces Halevi--Shoup rotations by up to 45.2\% on ResNet-32, and an adaptive mixed-degree allocation scheme lowers multiplicative depth from 66 to 56 levels, enabling leveled inference without bootstrapping.

</details>


### [150] [HALLMARK: Diagnosing Three Failure Modes in LLM Citation Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18360)
*Patrik Reizinger,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HALLMARK是一个包含2,526条BibTeX条目、14种幻觉类型、三种难度等级和六个诊断子测试的基准数据集，用于评估和比较文献验证系统。研究发现，假阳性率（而非召回率）是决定验证器是否可部署的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在学术写作中广泛应用，生成的参考文献幻觉问题日益严重（如NeurIPS 2025已发现53篇论文存在幻觉引用），但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同验证方法并提供详细的失败诊断。

Method: 构建HALLMARK基准：2,526条BibTeX条目、14种幻觉类型、三个难度等级、每个条目六个诊断子测试，以及抗污染的数据集划分。评估了DOI查找基线、零样本LLM、工具增强智能体和基于规则的验证器bibtex-updater。

Result: 核心发现：假阳性率（而非召回率）决定验证器是否可部署。三种具体失败模式：1）智能体查找提高召回但增加假阳性；2）在真实场景的基础率下，FPR的数量级差异决定了验证器的标记主要是真实发现还是噪声；3）多数LLM对超出训练截止日期的论文过度标记。最后提醒：FPR是部署瓶颈，但未检测到的伪造对科学记录而言仍是代价更高的错误。

Conclusion: HALLMARK提供了首个共享基准用于比较文献引用验证系统，明确指出假阳性率是实际部署的关键瓶颈，为未来验证器设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now routinely draft literature reviews and assist with academic writing, which means a higher risk of fabricated references: GPTZero found 53 papers with hallucinated citations among NeurIPS 2025's accepted set. Rule- and LLM-based verifiers are emerging, but no shared benchmark compares them and gives detailed failure diagnostics. We close that gap with HALLMARK (Hallucination benchmark): 2,526 BibTeX entries spanning 14 hallucination types, three difficulty tiers, six diagnostic sub-tests per entry, and a contamination-resistant held-out split. On it we evaluate a DOI-lookup baseline, frontier LLMs zero-shot, tool-augmented agents, and our own rule-based, co-designed verifier bibtex-updater. Across the benchmark one result is consistent: the false-positive rate, not recall, decides whether a verifier is deployable. HALLMARK makes it concrete through three failure modes: agentic lookups buy recall but inflate false positives; at a venue-realistic base rate, the order-of-magnitude spread in false-positive rates (FPRs) -- not recall -- governs whether a verifier's flags are mostly true catches or mostly noise; and most LLMs over-flag papers published past their training cutoff, where only the two latest-cutoff models hold their false-positive rate near in-distribution levels (a signal we report as descriptive, since it is confounded with possible recall of those entries). Thus FPR is the deployment bottleneck, but an undetected fabrication remains the costlier error for the scientific record.

</details>


### [151] [Adversarial Robustness of Phishing Email Detection: A Comparative Study of TF-IDF + Logistic Regression and Fine-Tuned DistilBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18429)
*Tanveer Ahmed,Seyedali Pourmoafil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对TF-IDF+逻辑回归和微调DistilBERT两种钓鱼邮件检测模型进行了受控对比实验，发现两者在清洁数据上准确率均超98%，但在对抗性测试中分别暴跌至64.00%和63.64%，差距仅0.36个百分点。可解释性分析表明两种模型依赖不同证据却表现出相似脆弱性，错误分析显示两者具有部分互补而非完全相同的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数钓鱼邮件检测模型的准确率报告基于清洁、同分布的测试数据，而非针对故意规避检测的对抗性样本，导致模型在真实对抗环境下的鲁棒性评估缺失。

Method: 构建包含82,255封邮件的统一语料库（来自6个公开数据集），采用TF-IDF+逻辑回归作为基线模型和微调DistilBERT作为深度模型，在正常同分布、合成钓鱼和对抗性钓鱼三种条件下进行评估，并使用LIME、SHAP和注意力展开分析进行可解释性分析。

Result: 清洁数据上两模型均超98%准确率；对抗测试中TF-IDF+LR降至64.00%（下降34.59个百分点），DistilBERT降至63.64%（下降35.40个百分点），差距仅0.36个百分点。两模型在54.9%的对抗样本上预测一致，但各有24和25个排他性错误。

Conclusion: 清洁数据准确率不能预测对抗鲁棒性，对抗性测试应成为钓鱼邮件检测评估的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Phishing emails remain one of the most persistent cybersecurity threats, and machine-learning classifiers are widely used to detect them. Most reported detection accuracies, however, are measured on clean, in-distribution test data rather than on emails deliberately altered to evade detection. This paper reports a controlled, pairwise comparison of two phishing-detection approaches a TF-IDF + Logistic Regression baseline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT transformer trained on a unified corpus of 82,255 emails drawn from six public datasets and evaluated under three conditions: normal in-distribution, synthetic phishing, and adversarial phishing. Both models exceeded 98% accuracy on clean data yet degraded sharply under adversarial testing: TF-IDF + LR fell to 64.00% (a 34.59-percentage-point drop) and DistilBERT fell to 63.64% (a 35.40-percentage-point drop) a gap of only 0.36 percentage points, equivalent to a single email in the 275-sample adversarial test set. LIME, SHAP, and attention-rollout analysis indicate the two models relied on different evidence yet showed similar vulnerability. Pairwise error analysis shows the models agreed on 54.9% of adversarial samples but each made a similar number of exclusive errors (24 and 25 respectively), indicating partly complementary rather than identical failure modes. The results show that clean-data accuracy does not predict adversarial robustness, and that adversarial testing should be a standard part of phishing-detection evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [ChainMark: Model-Free LLM Watermarking with Closed-Form Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18445)
*Chengheng Li-Chen,Kyuhee Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ChainMark提出了一种基于马尔可夫链的主动文本水印方法，通过密钥控制的SHA-256将词表划分为S个状态，并对部分位置强制施加硬马尔可夫转移。该方法无需访问生成模型，检测仅需O(n)次哈希运算，并给出了闭合形式的状态数选择公式（定理1）、普适鲁棒性阈值约29.3%（定理2）及其对k-正则拓扑的推广（定理3）。在三个指令微调LLM和四个领域上，ChainMark在翻译和随机替换攻击下严格优于KGW和SWEET；通过单语料库经验重校准可在自然语言文本上恢复1%的目标FPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印检测器依赖生成模型访问和启发式阈值，缺乏闭合形式的校准方法。欧盟AI法案等监管要求合成文本的机器可读标记，亟需一种无需LM访问、可理论校准且鲁棒性可证明的水印方案。

Method: 1) 通过密钥控制的SHA-256将词表划分为S个状态；2) 对比例rho的位置强制施加硬马尔可夫转移（状态间转移构成马尔可夫链）；3) 检测器使用相同密钥重放划分，仅需O(n)次哈希运算，无需访问LM；4) 理论推导了最小状态数S*(n, rho, alpha)（定理1）、普适鲁棒性阈值delta*≈29.3%（定理2）及其对k-正则拓扑的推广（定理3）。

Result: 在三个指令微调LLM和四个领域上，ChainMark在翻译和随机替换攻击下严格优于KGW和SWEET。通过单语料库经验重校准，可在自然语言文本上恢复1%的目标FPR。

Conclusion: ChainMark是一种无需LM访问、具有闭合形式校准和可证明鲁棒性阈值的主动文本水印方法，在多种攻击下优于现有方法，并能通过经验重校准实现准确的FPR控制。

Abstract: Regulatory regimes such as the EU AI Act mandate machine-readable marking of synthetic text, but existing watermark detectors rely on the generating LM and on heuristic thresholds with no closed-form calibration. We introduce ChainMark, an active watermark that partitions the vocabulary into S states via keyed SHA-256 and forces a hard Markov transition on a fraction rho of positions; the detector replays the partition from the same key in O(n) hash operations, with no LM access. We derive a closed-form S*(n, rho, alpha) mapping a target FPR, text length, and budget to the minimum state count (Theorem 1), prove a universal robustness threshold delta* = 1 - 1/sqrt(2) approximately 29.3% that is invariant in (S, rho, n) (Theorem 2), and generalise both to any k-regular transition topology (Theorem 3). Across three instruction-tuned LLMs and four domains, ChainMark strictly dominates KGW and SWEET under translation and random-substitution attacks at matched budget; a one-corpus empirical recalibration restores the 1% target FPR on natural-language text.

</details>


### [153] [0-Cyclic Equalizability of Binary Words Characterized by Hamming Weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18452)
*Sarunyu Thongjarast*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究二元牌面序列在仅允许插入0牌条件下的循环不可区分性问题，证明了两个等长二元词可0-循环等化当且仅当它们具有相同的汉明权重（即相同数量的1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在牌面密码学中，随机切牌使序列在循环移位下不可区分。先前研究允许任意插牌，本文则探索更严格的限制：仅允许插入0牌，考察两个二元序列能否通过匹配位置插0实现循环不可区分。

Method: 将一对二元词编码为四字母表{A, B, X, O}上的单一词，将0-循环等化问题约化为该编码下的更简单条件，并通过显式构造给出插牌方案。

Result: 两个等长二元词0-循环等化当且仅当它们汉明权重相等（即1的数量相同）。必要性显然，本文证明了充分性。

Conclusion: 在仅允许插入0牌的严格限制下，汉明权重相等是二元序列实现循环不可区分的充要条件。

Abstract: The random cut is one of the most fundamental shuffles in card-based cryptography: it rotates a sequence of face-down cards by a secret amount. Under this shuffle, two sequences of cards are indistinguishable if and only if they are cyclic shifts of each other. This motivates the question of whether, given two sequences of cards, inserting cards at matching positions can make them indistinguishable. A previous study shows that such an insertion is always possible when any cards may be inserted, as long as the two words are permutations of each other. This paper considers a stronger restriction: if the cards are binary, carrying only 0 or 1, can we insert only 0s to make the sequences indistinguishable? We call two words 0-cyclically equalizable if one can insert 0s into both sequences at matching positions so that the resulting words are cyclic shifts of each other. Our main result is that two binary words of equal length are 0-cyclically equalizable if and only if they have equal Hamming weight, that is, the same number of 1-bits. Since equal Hamming weight is clearly necessary, the content of the paper is to show that it is also sufficient. Our proof is constructive: we encode a pair of binary words as a single word over the four-letter alphabet {A, B, X, O}, reduce equalizability to a simpler condition in this encoding, and build the required insertion explicitly.

</details>


### [154] [Trusted Credentials, Untrusted Behavior: Benchmarking LLM-Agent Security in High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18485)
*Jie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义了HPC环境中LLM代理被劫持的威胁模型，分析了调度器、共享存储、多项目账户和科学工作流中的攻击面，指出现有控制措施的不足，并提出了研究议程和TaskBound基准测试计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有账户级安全控制无法防范LLM代理在执行授权操作时被对抗性指令劫持，这一问题在HPC领域尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过定义HPC环境下的威胁模型，系统识别调度器、共享存储、多项目账户和科学工作流等场景中的攻击面，并评估现有控制措施的局限性。

Result: 发现HPC现有安全控制（身份与隔离）无法表达具体任务的意图，使得代理在执行授权命令时可能被劫持。

Conclusion: 提出研究议程和TaskBound基准测试计划，旨在系统解决HPC中LLM代理被劫持的授权问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are starting to take on routine work in high-performance computing (HPC), including monitoring Slurm jobs, diagnosing failed builds, inspecting simulation output, and coordinating scientific workflows. To do this work, an agent commonly acts under its user's credentials and inherits the user's access to files and the scheduler. This arrangement creates a failure mode that ordinary account-level controls do not capture. Adversarial instructions in a log, tool description, shared file, or peer-agent message may redirect the agent beyond the task the user assigned, even though every resulting command is authenticated and permitted for that account. We refer to this as the hijacked authorized agent problem. Existing agent-security studies explain relevant mechanisms, such as indirect prompt injection and tool misuse, but generally evaluate them in web, enterprise, or personal-assistant settings. HPC security, by contrast, has mature controls for identity and isolation but does not ordinarily represent the intent of a particular task. This paper defines the threat model in the HPC setting, identifies attack surfaces created by schedulers, shared storage, multi-project accounts, and scientific workflows, and examines where current controls fall short. It concludes with a research agenda and a plan for an empirical benchmark, TaskBound.

</details>


### [155] [Towards an Automated Test of LLM Security Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18496)
*Shufan Chai,Liangliang Sun,Jessica Staddon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半自动方法，利用消费者保护机构（CPA）的权威信息来评估LLM在安全领域的知识差距，并在身份盗窃和冒名诈骗两个主题上对Gemini和GPT两个系列的5个模型进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全知识评估方法（如构建挑战问题集或任务基准）需要大量人工工作和安全专业知识。作者旨在开发一种可部分自动化的方法来高效识别LLM在安全领域的知识缺口。

Method: 利用消费者保护机构（CPA）的权威公开信息，通过检测LLM响应中的不稳定性来指示知识差距。在身份盗窃和冒名诈骗两个安全主题上，使用6个CPA的公开信息，对Gemini和GPT系列的5个LLM进行测试。

Result: 该方法能够区分哪些模型拥有足够知识来准确识别文本叙述中的安全主题，哪些模型存在知识缺口。

Conclusion: 提出的半自动方法可有效评估LLM在特定安全领域的知识掌握程度，减少了人工评估的工作量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for a range of software, hardware and human-centered security tasks. Consequently, LLM performance on security tasks is an active area of measurement and research, often with a focus on identifying areas in which LLM security ``knowledge'' may be insufficient. Popular strategies for identifying LLM security knowledge gaps include building corpora of challenge questions or task benchmarks, strategies that require substantial manual work and security expertise to design and execute. We introduce a partially-automated method for assessing LLM knowledge of a security area.The method uses authoritative information from Consumer Protection Agencies (CPAs) to identify instability in LLM responses that can be indicative of knowledge gaps. We demonstrate the method for 2 security topics, identity theft and impostor scams, and 5 LLMs in 2 leading LLM families, Gemini and GPT, using publicly available information about identity theft and impostor scams from 6 CPAs.The method distinguishes between models that have and don't have sufficient knowledge to accurately identify the security topics in text narratives.

</details>


### [156] [CryptanalysisBench: Can LLMs do Cryptanalysis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18538)
*Lukas Fluri,Avital Shafran,Nicholas Carlini,Matthew Jagielski,Milad Nasr,Orr Dunkelman,Eyal Ronen,Florian Tramèr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CryptanalysisBench基准，包含191个密码分析任务，测试前沿LLM的密码分析能力。结果显示，五个前沿模型能攻破65%-86%的Tier 1方案，并发现了此前未知的密码分析攻击（如SpoC AEAD和KINDI的漏洞），表明LLM在密码分析领域的能力正在迅速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码分析处于数学推理与网络安全的交叉点，是测试前沿推理能力的理想试验场，且密码原语的安全性对数字安全至关重要。作者希望探究LLM是否具备密码分析能力，并建立一个标准化基准来追踪AI密码分析的发展进程。

Method: 构建CryptanalysisBench基准，包含191个任务，覆盖6类密码原语家族（分组密码、哈希函数等），主要来自4个NIST标准化竞赛。基准分为三个层级：(i)已知存在实际攻破方案的原语；(ii)尚无已知实际攻破方案的原语（含全强度和缩微变体）；(iii)处于密码分析前沿的生产级原语挑战集。使用五个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 5, Mythos 5, GPT 5.5, GLM 5.2）进行测试。

Result: 前沿模型攻破了65%-86%的Tier 1方案、6-12个全强度Tier 2方案以及24-61个缩微变体。模型还产生了此前未知的密码分析结果，包括利用SpoC AEAD设计缺陷的密钥恢复攻击和KINDI已发布CCA安全性证明中的错误。

Conclusion: LLM在密码分析领域的能力正在快速提升，可能很快（在某些方面已经）达到甚至超过已发表的最先进水平。CryptanalysisBench可作为追踪AI密码分析能力的工具，以及候选方案部署前的压力测试框架。

Abstract: Cryptanalysis - the task of finding attacks against cryptographic schemes - sits at the intersection of mathematical reasoning and cybersecurity, two areas where LLMs have advanced fastest. Cryptanalysis represents both a clean testbed for frontier reasoning (as practical attacks can be automatically verified) and a domain with unusually high stakes, since the primitives under study underpin our digital security. In this paper we ask whether LLMs can do cryptanalysis, and find that the answer is increasingly yes.
  We introduce CryptanalysisBench, 191 tasks across six families of cryptographic primitives (block ciphers, hash functions, etc.) drawn primarily from four NIST standardization competitions. Our benchmark consists of three tiers: (i) primitives with known practical breaks; (ii) primitives with no known practical break, evaluated both at full strength and as scaled-down variants; and (iii) a challenge set of production primitives at the frontier of cryptanalysis.
  Five frontier models (Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 5, Mythos 5, GPT 5.5, and the open-weights GLM 5.2) break 65%-86% of Tier 1 schemes, 6-12 Tier-2 schemes at full strength, and 24-61 across all scaled-down variants. Beyond deriving known results, models produce novel cryptanalysis, such as a key-recovery attack that exploits a design flaw in the SpoC AEAD and an error in KINDI's published CCA-security proof, both to the best of our knowledge not previously known.
  We release CryptanalysisBench as a tool to help track if (or when) AI cryptanalysis becomes a serious factor and as a scaffold for stress-testing candidate schemes before deployment. The attacks that the benchmark already surfaces are an early snapshot of a fast-moving frontier that may soon match, and in places exceed, the published state of the art.

</details>


### [157] [RECEIPT: Deterministic, Reward-Hacking-Resistant Verification for White-Box Agentic XSS Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18575)
*Muxi Lyu,Karen Shieh,Yiwei Hou,Hao Wang,Koushik Sen,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RECEIPT是一个针对LLM代码代理发现的XSS漏洞的验证框架，通过环境隔离、PoC约束、角色分离和判定绑定四个机制，确保代理报告的漏洞可被可靠复现。在95个真实应用上，花费20美元/应用发现了24个未知XSS漏洞，其中12个已获维护者确认，且零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代码代理在XSS漏洞发现中展现了潜力，但代理的漏洞声明不可直接信任，存在奖励篡改（reward-hacking）行为。现有方法（代理自我判断、黑盒扫描）存在误报或漏报问题，亟需一个可靠的验证框架来确认代理报告的XSS漏洞是否真实有效。

Method: RECEIPT框架实施四项约束：(1) 环境隔离——确保验证环境与代理探索环境分离；(2) PoC约束——要求PoC在真实浏览器中执行且payload被成功植入；(3) 角色分离——区分攻击者角色（植入payload）和受害者角色（执行payload）；(4) 判定绑定——将验证结果与具体执行过程绑定，使验证过程确定性和可复现。

Result: 在95个真实开源Web应用上，20美元/应用的预算内，RECEIPT发现24个未知XSS漏洞（12个已获维护者确认），在已知漏洞恢复任务中36%成功复现标注CVE。与代理自判断和黑盒扫描器相比，RECEIPT确认了更多真实漏洞且零误报。

Conclusion: RECEIPT通过约束性回放验证机制，有效解决了LLM代理在XSS发现中的可信问题，实现了高准确率、零误报的漏洞验证，证明了基于LLM的漏洞发现结合严谨验证是可行的自动化安全测试方案。

Abstract: Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) remains one of the most prevalent and damaging classes of web vulnerabilities. LLM-based coding agents offer a promising approach to XSS discovery by combining source-code reasoning with interactive testing against a running application. However, a coding agent's claims cannot be trusted on their own. We characterize three reward-hacking behaviors in white-box agentic XSS discovery and propose three requirements that an ideal verifier should meet.
  We present RECEIPT, a verification framework that makes agent-reported XSS findings trustworthy by enforcing environment isolation, PoC constraints, role separation, and verdict binding. Each confirmation therefore establishes two properties: the script runs in a real browser, and the payload was planted under the attacker role and executed in the victim role's browser. This constrained replay procedure makes validation deterministic and reproducible. We evaluate RECEIPT on 95 real-world web-application targets drawn from popular open-source projects. Within a $20 per-application budget, RECEIPT found 24 previously unknown XSS vulnerabilities, 12 of which have already been acknowledged by maintainers after responsible disclosure, and recovered the labeled CVE in 36% of known-vulnerability recovery targets. Compared with the same agent using self-judgment and with black-box scanners, RECEIPT confirms more real exploits while admitting no false positives.

</details>


### [158] [CPInj: Uncovering Prompt Injection Risks in Textual Collaborative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18622)
*Xinting Liao,Behnoosh Zamanlooy,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,David B. Emerson,Ruinan Jin,Deval Pandya,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了文本协作提示优化（TCPO）中存在的新型攻击面——恶意指令可通过协作优化循环注入并传播，提出了CPInj注入攻击方法，发现现有防御无效，并提出了APAgg防御方法，但攻击威胁仍远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCPO将提示优化扩展到去中心化场景，但其基于自由文本更新和聚合的机制引入了新的攻击面：恶意指令可被注入本地提示并通过服务器端聚合传播。现有研究尚未探索这一漏洞，亟需揭示风险并提出初步防御。

Method: 提出CPInj攻击方法，针对TCPO协作优化循环注入恶意指令；提出APAgg防御方法，通过面向防御的聚合净化恶意指令并恢复TCPO效用。在三种LLM家族和五项推理任务上进行了实验验证。

Result: CPInj能成功污染全局提示、降低下游任务性能、抵抗良性客户端的提示优化净化并规避基于检测的防御；现有防御方法对CPInj无效；APAgg能部分缓解攻击但仍未完全解决问题。

Conclusion: TCPO存在严重安全漏洞，CPInj攻击揭示了这一关键风险；APAgg迈出了防御的第一步但远不足以完全抵御攻击，亟需更鲁棒的防御机制。

Abstract: Textual Collaborative Prompt Optimization (TCPO) extends Textgrad (Yuksekgonul et al., 2025) to a decentralized setting by allowing multiple clients to jointly improve prompts for large language models (LLMs) while keeping their data locally. Its reliance on free-form textual updating and aggregation introduces a new and largely unexplored attack surface, i.e., malicious instructions can be injected into local prompts and propagated through server-side prompt aggregation. Unlike conventional prompt injection attacks, attacking TCPO targets the collaborative optimization loop in TCPO. This setting is more challenging because malicious instructions must survive aggregation, persist through subsequent benign prompt optimization, and evade server-side defenses. To expose this risk, we propose CPInj, a collaborative prompt injection attack that contaminates the aggregated global prompt with malicious instructions, degrades downstream task performance, resists purification by prompt optimization on benign clients, and evades advanced detection-based defenses on the server. We find that current defense methods are ineffective against CPInj. To mitigate this attack, we further propose a defense-oriented aggregation method, i.e., APAgg, which purifies malicious instructions and partially recovers TCPO utility. We conduct extensive experiments across three LLM families and five reasoning tasks in math, logic, and medicine. The results demonstrate that our proposed attack reveals a critical vulnerability in TCPO. Although we take a first step toward mitigation, the attack remains highly effective and far from fully resolved, calling for more robust defense for TCPO.

</details>


### [159] [Broken Gates: Re-evaluating Web Bot Defenses in the Age of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18659)
*Behzad Ousat,Nikita Turkmen,Lalchandra Rampersaud,Dillan Bailey,Amin Kharraz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM浏览器代理对现有机器人防御系统的有效性，发现基于挑战的防御（如hCaptcha、reCaptcha v2）极易被商业验证码破解服务绕过，非交互式防御（如reCaptcha v3）的抵抗力主要源于执行环境真实性而非代理行为本身，安全边界位于环境层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的浏览器代理能够自主导航、推理页面内容并用自然语言与网页交互，这从根本上改变了Web安全威胁格局，挑战了现有机器人管理系统的有效性。现有研究缺乏对两类防御（交互式挑战防御与非交互式信任防御）面对LLM代理和商业破解服务时的系统性测量评估。

Method: 针对两类攻击者（商业验证码破解服务和LLM浏览器代理），跨越7个破解服务商和6个代理配置（云托管、自托管、AI辅助、浏览器扩展），在hCaptcha、reCaptcha v2、reCaptcha v3和Cloudflare Turnstile四种防御系统上进行系统测量实验。通过细粒度交互轨迹分析，隔离行为差异与环境差异对防御结果的影响。

Result: 基于挑战的防御对商业破解服务几乎无效（近100%绕过，成本极低）。LLM代理配备专用求解模块时也可击败挑战防御。非交互式防御（reCaptcha v3）表现出更强抵抗力，但该抵抗力并非源于安全属性的根本差异。两个行为足迹几乎无法区分的代理在reCaptcha v3上结果截然不同（一个绕过，一个失败），决定性因素是执行环境真实性而非代理行为。

Conclusion: 非交互式防御的安全边界位于环境层而非行为层，这对机器人管理系统的设计与评估有重要启示。现有防御体系面临根本性挑战。

Abstract: LLM-based browser agents are rapidly changing the threat landscape for web security. Unlike traditional automation frameworks that execute predefined scripts, these agents can autonomously navigate websites, reason about page content, and interact with web interfaces using natural-language instructions. This evolution raises fundamental questions about the effectiveness of bot management systems, widely deployed to defend against automated web abuse. In this paper, we present a systematic measurement study evaluating the resilience of both interactive challenge-based defenses and non-interactive trust-based defenses against two attacker classes: commercial Captcha-solving services and LLM-based browser agents. Our evaluation spans seven solver services and six agents, including cloud-hosted, self-hosted, AI-assisted, and browser-extension configurations, tested against hCaptcha, reCaptcha v2, reCaptcha v3, and Cloudflare Turnstile. Our results show that challenge-based defenses are broadly ineffective against commercial solvers, which achieve near-perfect bypass at negligible cost. The challenges can similarly be defeated by LLM-based agents when a dedicated solver module is available. Non-interactive defenses such as reCaptcha v3 exhibit stronger resistance, but our analysis reveals that this resilience does not reflect a fundamental security property. Through fine-grained interaction trace analysis, we find that two agents with nearly indistinguishable behavioral footprints yield divergent outcomes, one bypassing the defense and one failing, isolating execution-environment authenticity, rather than agent behavior, as the determining factor. These findings suggest that the security boundary of non-interactive defenses lies at the environment layer, with significant implications for how bot management systems are designed and evaluated.

</details>


### [160] [When to Trust the Map: Confidence-Aware LLM Routing for Automotive CVE-to-ATM Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18684)
*Heeyun Heo,Sangmin Park,Huy Kang Kim,Sanghoon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于置信度感知的选择性自动化框架，将CVE描述到Auto-ISAC攻击矩阵的映射问题转化为分级决策问题。通过层级上下文学习生成候选映射，融合自一致性与LLM证据检验信号，利用校准后的置信度将结果分为自动确认、人工审核或搁置三类。高置信模式下自动档位精度达0.878，AUROC为0.868。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVE描述与Auto-ISAC攻击矩阵之间存在语义鸿沟，直接映射在安全关键场景下可能导致威胁解读错误和优先级误判，因此需要一种能区分可靠与不确定映射的置信度感知方法。

Method: 将CVE到ATM的映射重构为选择性自动化问题：首先通过层级上下文学习生成候选映射，然后融合自一致性机制和基于LLM的证据验证信号，构建校准元模型，输出校准置信度分数，并据此将候选映射路由至AUTO（自动确认）、REVIEW（人工审核）或HOLD（搁置）三类。

Result: 相比Flat零样本GPT-5.2基线，候选集精度在匹配召回率下显著提升。高置信模式下AUTO档位精度达0.878，为候选集基线率的两倍以上；校准置信度区分正确与错误候选的AUROC达0.868。

Conclusion: 该框架能够通过隔离可自动确认的映射与需分析师审核的映射，有效支持选择性自动化，降低安全关键场景中的误映射风险。

Abstract: Public CVE descriptions report the technical conditions and impact of vulnerabilities, whereas the Auto-ISAC Automotive Threat Matrix (ATM) expresses an adversary's tactics and techniques. Because the two representations are not directly aligned, incorrect automated mappings in safety-critical environments may distort threat interpretation and mitigation prioritization, motivating a confidence-aware approach that distinguishes auto-confirmable mappings from uncertain cases. This paper reformulates automotive CVE-to-ATM mapping as a selective automation problem. The proposed framework generates candidate mappings via hierarchical in-context learning, then fuses self-consistency and LLM-based evidence verification signals into a calibrated meta-model. The resulting calibrated confidence score routes each candidate into AUTO, REVIEW, or HOLD. On the evaluation set, the proposed system substantially improved candidate-set precision at matched recall over a Flat zero-shot GPT-5.2 baseline. In the High-Confidence operating mode, the AUTO tier achieved a precision of 0.878, more than double the candidate-set base rate, and the calibrated confidence score achieved an AUROC of 0.868 in distinguishing correct from incorrect candidates. These results show that the framework can support selective automation by isolating auto-confirmable mappings from those requiring analyst review.

</details>


### [161] [GLID: Gated Local Intrinsic Dimension Repairs the Blind Spots of Face-Forgery Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18770)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GLID通过几何信号（局部内在维度）修复微调检测器对未见生成器家族的盲区，在跨生成器基准上达到0.805平均AUC，并发现两个经验规律：伪造图像在特定层弯曲令牌流形，且几何信号在训练数据覆盖不足时起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微调基础模型检测器在已知生成器上表现优异，但对训练中未出现的生成器家族完全失效。作者希望用几何信号而非数据来修复这一盲区。

Method: GLID将单张图像的patch tokens视为流形上的样本，在冻结的ViT多个深度层估计局部内在维度（LID），得到12维信号。该信号通过置信门控与微调检测器结合，门控强度仅用分布内数据校准。

Result: 在16轴跨生成器基准上，GLID达到0.805平均AUC，优于重新训练的最强基线。生成轴提升+0.084 AUC，而重演轴仅下降-0.005。跨种子的精度标准差降低5.5倍。

Conclusion: 发现了两个经验规律：1）伪造图像在不同生成器家族的不同深度弯曲令牌流形（GAN在最后一层，扩散在中间层），该模式跨骨干网络、维度估计器和非人脸图像稳定；2）微调在训练数据覆盖处吸收辅助增益，注入1%目标家族图像即消除+0.100增益，证明几何信号恰好在数据不可用处起关键作用。

Abstract: Fine-tuned foundation-model detectors dominate face-forgery benchmarks, yet they stay blind to generator families absent from training. We present GLID, a detector that repairs this blind spot with geometry instead of data. GLID treats the patch tokens of a single image as a sample from a manifold and estimates their local intrinsic dimension (LID) at several depths of a frozen vision transformer. This 12-dimensional, training-free signal enters a fine-tuned detector through a confidence gate whose strength is calibrated purely in-distribution. On a 16-axis cross-generator benchmark, GLID reaches 0.805 mean AUC, first among retrained state-of-the-art baselines and never significantly behind the strongest of them on any axis. It lifts the generation axes by +0.084 AUC while moving reenactment by only -0.005. Two empirical laws explain the design. First, forged faces bend the token manifold at family-specific depths: GAN artifacts peak at the last layer, diffusion artifacts peak mid-network, and the pattern survives four backbones, three dimension estimators, and non-face imagery. Second, fine-tuning absorbs auxiliary gains exactly where training data covers: injecting 1% target-family images erases a +0.100 gain, so geometric signals matter precisely where data is unavailable. The deterministic signal also cuts the cross-seed spread of accuracy 5.5x. Code, preregistered analysis gates, and per-image scores accompany the paper.

</details>


### [162] [Cross-Agent Campaign Attribution: Linking Asynchronous Attacks Across LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18826)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨智能体异步对抗归因问题，并设计A²FV指纹向量方法，在SCD-v1基准上实现0.82的成对AUC，证明跨会话、跨代理的对抗攻击溯源可行且不可简化为单一风格特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体防御通常按单次会话评估，但在实际部署中，攻击可跨独立智能体、团队和运行时分发，导致每个本地防护仅看到零散片段。需要一种能够在没有共享运行时状态、测试时攻击标签或攻击者身份预言的情况下，将来自同一潜在对抗活动的会话关联起来的归因方法。

Method: 提出异步归因指纹向量（A²FV），一种轻量级代理侧参考协议，通过从代理可观察的工具使用、时序和提示残留特征中计算成对会话相似度来链接攻击会话。同时构建了SCD-v1基准，包含良性流量、隔离攻击、多会话活动、匹配的非预言规避和泄漏审计。

Result: 在SCD-v1上，A²FV的成对归因AUC达到0.82，而基于得分的单会话检测器适配和分块LLM评判器在该任务上接近随机水平。最强的固定信号来自结构和文体残留特征，时序则作为更丰富代理追踪的诊断通道。交叉风格控制表明信号部分对风格敏感但不完全可归约为风格。

Conclusion: 跨智能体活动归因应作为保护真实环境中LLM智能体的独立评估层，现有单会话检测器不足以应对跨会话、跨代理的分布式攻击场景。

Abstract: LLM-agent defenses are typically evaluated one session at a time. In deployment, however, attacks can be distributed across independent agents, teams, and runtimes, leaving each local guardrail with only a sparse fragment. We formalize cross-agent asynchronous campaign attribution: linking sessions from the same latent adversarial campaign without shared runtime state, test-time campaign labels, or attacker identity oracles. We introduce Asynchronous Attribution Fingerprint Vectors ($A^2FV$), a lightweight proxy-side reference protocol for scoring pairwise campaign similarity from proxy-observable tool-use, timing, and prompt residue. We also construct SCD-v1, a controlled persona-matched benchmark with benign traffic, isolated attacks, multi-session campaigns, matched non-oracle evasion, and leakage audits. On SCD-v1, $A^2FV$ achieves 0.82 pairwise AUC for campaign linking, while score-only adaptations of per-session detectors and chunked LLM judges remain near chance under the same task. The strongest fixed signal is carried by structural and stylometric residue, while timing is retained as a diagnostic channel for richer proxy traces. Crossed-style controls show that the signal is partly style-sensitive but not reducible to style alone. Static and dimension-aware non-oracle stress tests further show that pairwise separability persists under controlled evasion. These results establish cross-agent campaign attribution as a distinct evaluation layer for securing LLM agents in the wild.

</details>


### [163] [Data Leakage Prevention in Agentic Applications via Preemptive Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18847)
*Akansha Shukla,Emily Bellov,Parth Atulbhai Gandhi,Yuval Elovici,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体系统的预部署安全流水线，通过扫描提示模板、工具接口和调用代码来识别数据泄露模式，并生成最小侵入性补丁（如模式收紧、边界清理、工具门控和最小权限检查），再通过对抗性提示注入攻击和良性输入变体进行验证。在五个真实应用和AgentDojo基准测试中，该方法在基本越狱和指令覆盖攻击下实现100%泄露消除，在压力诱导操纵下减少91%泄露，且无需持续的运行时策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将LLM驱动的规划与外部工具接口集成，但指令/数据边界失效和提示注入攻击使得数据泄露和工具误用成为可能。在跨多个代码库和异构代理的工作流中，一致执行所需控制尤为困难，现有方案缺乏系统性的预部署安全验证方法。

Method: 提出一个三阶段预部署流水线：1）扫描阶段分析提示模板、工具接口和工具调用代码，识别泄露模式；2）加固阶段优先处理高风险工具，应用最小侵入性缓解措施（模式收紧、边界清理、基于允许列表的工具门控、最小权限检查）；3）验证阶段自动生成对抗性攻击输入（越狱、指令覆盖、工具定向操纵）和良性任务变体，确认修复后功能正常。

Result: 在五个真实世界智能体应用和AgentDojo基准测试中，流水线识别了重复出现的泄露模式并生成不影响应用行为的补丁。基本越狱和指令覆盖攻击下泄露减少100%，压力诱导操纵下泄露减少91%，且无需运行时策略持续执行。

Conclusion: 该预部署流水线能有效消除多智能体系统中的数据泄露漏洞，通过静态分析+自动加固+对抗性验证的组合方式，在不破坏功能的前提下实现高安全性，减少了对运行时策略的依赖。

Abstract: Agentic systems integrate LLM driven planning with interfaces to external tools, making data leakage and tool misuse feasible via instruction/data boundary failures and prompt injection attacks. Enforcing required controls consistently is particularly challenging in workflows spanning many codebases and heterogeneous agents. To address this challenge in multi agentic systems, we present a pre-deployment pipeline for scanning, hardening, and validation of agentic applications. The pipeline analyzes prompt templates, tool interfaces, and tool-invocation code to identify leakage-enabling patterns and generate actionable patches. The hardened application is then validated through adversarial prompt injection attacks and benign input variations ensuring that mitigations do not disrupt intended behavior. In the hardening stage, high-risk tools are prioritized, and minimally invasive mitigations are applied, including schema tightening, boundary sanitization, allowlist-based tool gating, and least-privilege checks. In the validation stage, the pipeline automatically generates attack inputs that mimic jailbreaks, instruction overrides, and tool-targeted manipulation, along with benign task variants, to confirm that the functionality of the hardened application is preserved after remediation. We evaluated the pipeline on five real-world agentic applications, as well as on the AgentDojo benchmark. Across all applications, the proposed pipeline identified recurring leakage-enabling patterns and generated patches that can be integrated without disrupting the intended application behavior. The resulting modifications of application code were shown to eliminate leaks when targeted by basic jailbreak and instruction-override attacks, achieving a 100% reduction in leakage, and reduce leaks by 91% under conditions of stress-induced manipulation, without the need of continuous runtime policy enforcement.

</details>


### [164] [Tracing the Shadows: Automatic Tracking and Analysis of Crypto Money Laundering via Transaction Semantic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18869)
*Hao Wu,Haijun Wang,Shangwang Li,Yin Wu,Ming Fan,Ting Liu,Xiapu Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMLGuard，一种面向账户型区块链的语义感知反洗钱框架，通过结合静态规则分析与检索增强的大语言模型推理，将复杂DeFi交易转化为高级语义表征（DeFi语义单元DSU），实现单链和跨链场景下准确、持续的非法资金流追踪。在82个真实洗钱案例（涉案资产超10亿美元）上，AMLGuard在单链和跨链数据集上分别实现了94.4%/87.6%的目的地精度、98.4%/95.8%的地址召回率和94.1%/93.8%的目的地召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反洗钱方法难以应对DeFi交易的语义复杂性，要么过度依赖底层代币转移，要么进行与协议无关的资金流分析，无法捕捉交易的高层意图。

Method: AMLGuard结合静态规则分析与检索增强的大语言模型推理，推断隐式DeFi语义；对跨链交易进行参数解析以恢复跨链语义；将每笔交易抽象为DeFi语义单元（DSU）。

Result: 在82个真实洗钱案例（涉案资产超10亿美元）上，单链/跨链数据集的目的地精度为94.4%/87.6%，地址召回率为98.4%/95.8%，目的地召回率为94.1%/93.8%。

Conclusion: AMLGuard能够有效应对DeFi交易的语义复杂性，实现准确、连续的资金洗钱追踪，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of decentralized finance (DeFi), security incidents related to cryptocurrency have become increasingly prevalent. After such incidents, attackers typically attempt to rapidly move stolen assets, concealing the origin of illicit funds and ultimately converting them into fiat currency. However, existing anti-money laundering (AML) methods struggle to cope with the semantic complexity of DeFi transactions. They either rely heavily on low-level token transfers, or perform protocol-agnostic money flow analysis, failing to capture the high-level intent of transactions.
  In this paper, we propose AMLGuard, a semantic-aware AML framework for account-based blockchains. AMLGuard tracks illicit fund flows from known malicious addresses by performing semantic analysis on complex DeFi transactions, enabling accurate and continuous laundering tracking. Given a complex transaction, AMLGuard combines static rule-based analysis with retrieval-augmented large language model (LLM) reasoning to infer implicit DeFi semantics, transforming raw transaction data into high-level semantic representations. Furthermore, for cross-chain transactions where laundering intent is not explicitly exposed, AMLGuard parses transaction parameters and performs argument parsing to recover cross-chain semantics, enabling seamless tracking across ledgers. Based on the inferred semantics, AMLGuard abstracts each transaction into a DeFi Semantic Unit (DSU). We evaluate the effectiveness of AMLGuard on 82 real-world laundering cases, involving illicit assets worth over $1 billion. Specifically, AMLGuard reconstructs compact illicit fund-flow topologies with destination precision of 94.4% and 87.6%, while achieving the highest address recall of 98.4% and 95.8% and destination recall of 94.1% and 93.8% on single-chain and cross-chain datasets.

</details>


### [165] [Chi-MERA: Defending Orbit-Based Authentication of LEO Satellites with the Space Oddity of MLAT (Long Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19047)
*Sarah Jung,Eric Jedermann,Martin Strohmeier,Jens Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现现有LEO卫星物理层认证方案易受多设备攻击（FPR高达40%），提出Chi-MERA方案，通过多边定位（MLAT）和弹性签名机制实现低FPR（<2%）和低FNR（<3%），且防御能力随接收器数量线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LEO卫星物理层认证方案在多设备攻击者面前存在脆弱性，误报率高达40%，亟需一种能够抵抗多设备攻击的鲁棒认证方案。

Method: 提出Chi-MERA方案，包含两项增强：(1) 利用多边定位（MLAT）区分轨道信号（合法卫星）与非轨道信号（攻击者），通过残差分析分类信号源类别；(2) 设计不依赖单一专用参考接收机的弹性签名机制。

Result: Chi-MERA方案实现FPR<2%，FNR<3%；防御能力线性扩展：n个接收器可抵抗n/2个设备的欺骗攻击（FPR<1%）。

Conclusion: Chi-MERA方案通过MLAT和弹性签名有效解决了LEO卫星物理层认证中的多设备攻击问题，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: An active research area, physical layer security can be a last resort in insecure legacy systems, a resource-efficient alternative, or a complementary backup for cryptographic techniques. In this paper, we demonstrate that previously established authentication schemes for LEO satellites are vulnerable to attackers that control multiple devices. In extensive experiments, such attackers produce false positive rates (FPR) of up to 40%.
  Based on a root cause analysis, we develop a novel scheme, called Chi-MERA, that improves authentication performance and is robust against multi-device attackers. Our scheme uses two enhancements: (1) multilateration (MLAT) to clearly distinguish between attackers and legitimate satellites, and (2), a new resilient signature scheme without dependency on a single dedicated reference receiver. Our evaluation shows that exploiting MLAT characteristics such as residual analysis to classify vastly different signal source categories (orbit vs. non-orbit) is highly effective. In extensive simulations, we show that the new authentication achieves low FPR of $<$2% and false negative rates of $<$3% for accidentally rejecting valid signals. Furthermore, our scheme's defense scales linearly: $n$ receivers reliably withstand spoofing (FPR $< 1%$) from attackers with $\frac{n}{2}$ devices.

</details>


### [166] [Exploiting Load/Store Leakage of Sparse Vectors for Key Recovery in HQC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19109)
*Gustavo Banegas,Benjamin Smith,Jad Zahreddine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对HQC（汉明准循环）密钥封装机制的侧信道攻击，利用编译器生成的寄存器溢出导致64位字的低32位和高32位泄露强度不对称，从电磁测量中区分秘密向量的零/非零字，将密钥恢复问题简化为缩短的综合征解码问题。对HQC-1参数集，约88.7%的机器字为零，解码复杂度降至约2^46比特操作，实验在Cortex-M4上验证了该攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQC已被NIST选为标准化候选方案，其抵抗实现攻击的能力至关重要。现有研究表明基于编码的密码方案对侧信道攻击存在脆弱性，但针对HQC的具体攻击尚不充分，需要评估其在实际硬件平台上的安全性。

Method: 分析Cortex-M4上参考实现生成的汇编代码，发现编译器因寄存器溢出导致64位字的低32位和高32位在内存访问时泄露强度不同；利用电磁测量构建零词区分器，识别秘密向量的零字位置；将零位置信息作为解码提示，将密钥恢复转化为缩短的综合征解码问题。

Result: 在HQC-1参数集下，32位粒度时约88.7%的机器字为零，解码复杂度降至约2^46比特操作；实验验证了低半通道约500条迹线、高半通道约5000条迹线的泄露不对称性，成功恢复HQC-1密钥的零字位置。

Conclusion: HQC在Cortex-M4上的实现存在由编译器优化引入的侧信道泄露，可被利用进行密钥恢复攻击。提出了消除攻击所利用的稀疏性的实用对策，为HQC的安全部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Hamming Quasi-Cyclic (HQC) is a code-based key encapsulation mechanism selected by NIST for standardization, making its resistance to implementation attacks critically important. We present a side-channel attack that exploits load/store leakage in the manipulation of HQC's sparse secret vectors. Analysing Cortex-M4 assembly generated from the reference implementation, we identify a leakage surface in which the low and high 32-bit halves of each 64-bit word leak with different strengths, due to compiler-generated register spilling. We exploit this leakage to construct a simple zero-word distinguisher classifying machine words of the secret vector as zero or nonzero from electromagnetic measurements. The recovered zero positions are then translated into decoding hints, reducing HQC key recovery to a shortened syndrome-decoding problem. We analyse the resulting decoding complexity for all HQC parameter sets: at 32-bit granularity an expected 88.7% of the machine words of y are zero for HQC-1, cutting the decoding to $\approx$ 2 46 bit operations. Experiments on a Cortex-M4 validate the predicted low/high-half asymmetry-approximately 500 traces for the stronger low-half channel and 5,000 for the weaker high-half channeland recover the zero words of an HQC-1 key at 32-bit granularity. Finally, we discuss practical countermeasures that eliminate the sparsity exploited by the attack.

</details>


### [167] [Sarus: Privacy-Preserving Multi-Vendor Perception Fusion via Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19146)
*Munawar Hasan,Apostol Vassilev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sarus是一个基于同态加密的隐私保护框架，用于多供应商自动驾驶感知融合。各供应商将检测结果编码为高斯矩向量并在加密域中聚合，实验表明该方法在KITTI数据集上能有效提升场景覆盖，且实时部署可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多供应商部署的协同感知需要在推理时共享检测输出，但这会暴露专有模型行为和敏感环境信息，存在严重的隐私与安全风险。

Method: 采用同态加密（HE）实现隐私保护感知融合：各供应商将检测结果编码为紧凑的高斯矩向量并通过共享空间格点传输加密载荷，融合服务器在加密域直接聚合，最后解密并通过类级桶合并重构最终检测结果。

Result: 计算复杂度分析显示供应商载荷构建线性扩展，服务器端融合为O(BV)，后处理为O(B+∑B_c²)。实验表明实际中呈线性扩展，HE仅带来有界常数倍开销，解密主导后处理成本。KITTI数据集实验证明Sarus能有效聚合互补检测结果提升场景级覆盖。

Conclusion: 当统计压缩与空间稀疏性被联合利用时，隐私保护的多供应商感知融合在实时部署中是可行的。

Abstract: Cooperative perception enables autonomous vehicles (AVs) to improve situational awareness by aggregating detection outputs from multiple agents and sensing platforms, often via a shared fusion service in multi-vendor deployments. However, sharing such outputs at inference time exposes proprietary model behavior and sensitive environmental information, creating significant privacy and security concerns. In this paper, we present Sarus, a privacy-preserving framework for multi-vendor perception fusion via homomorphic encryption (HE), enabling aggregation without revealing individual vendor outputs. Each vendor encodes detections as compact Gaussian moment vectors over a shared spatial lattice and transmits encrypted payloads to a fusion server, which aggregates them directly in the encrypted domain. The fused result is then decrypted and reconstructed into final detections through class-wise bin merging.
  We analyze the computational complexity, showing linear scaling for vendor payload construction and $O(BV)$ server-side fusion with the number of occupied bins $B$ and vendors $V$, while postprocessing scales as $O(B + \sum_{c\in \mathcal{C}} B_c^2)$, where $\mathcal{C}$ denotes the set of object classes and $B_c$ is the number of occupied bins for class $c$. Experiments demonstrate linear scaling in practice with only a bounded constant-factor overhead from HE, with decryption dominating postprocessing cost. Experiments on the KITTI dataset using camera (YOLOv8) and LiDAR (PointPillars, PV-RCNN) detectors show that Sarus improves scene-level coverage by effectively aggregating complementary detections, particularly in distance-dependent regimes where individual modalities degrade. These results indicate that privacy-preserving multi-vendor perception fusion is feasible for real-time deployment when statistical compression and spatial sparsity are jointly exploited.

</details>


### [168] [PTSan: A Practical Memory Safety Sanitizer for C/C++ with Pointer-Object Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19246)
*Eli Davis,Eric Lahtinen,Michael Gordon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PTSan是一种基于LLVM的指针化内存安全检测工具，通过将对象标识符存储在指针高位、边界信息存储在固定大小的运行时表中，在保持低开销、优化器可见性和硬件兼容性的同时，实现了与最快速的位置化检测工具相当的运行时开销（SPEC CPU 2017上57.2%的几何平均开销），并保留了跨对象、非对象和时间维度的内存错误检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++中内存安全错误仍然是严重漏洞的主要来源。指针化检测工具相比位置化工具（如ASan）能提供更强的安全保障，但其过高的开销和兼容性问题阻碍了生产环境部署。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，实现实用的、可部署于生产环境的指针化内存安全检测。

Method: PTSan的核心设计是将对象标识符（ID）存储在指针的高位比特中，对象边界存储在固定大小的运行时表中。这种表示方法用有限的活动对象数量预算换取低开销、优化器可见性和对商用硬件的支持。标识符随指针值传播，LLVM数据流分析即可自动传播而无需额外元数据指令。将元数据查找与检查执行分离，两者均暴露为LLVM IR，支持检查指令的提降、消除和合并，以及基于最小割的兼容性标签移除放置。在支持Intel LAM的硬件上可进一步消除标签操作。

Result: 在SPEC CPU 2017上，x86-64几何平均开销57.2%（启用Intel LAM后46.4%），ARM64为54.7%；在LLVM MultiSource套件上x86-64开销31.5%。物理内存开销接近原生水平。在独立内存安全测试套件中，保持了跨对象、非对象和时域检测覆盖率。在实际服务器和安全工作负载上展示了实用的开销水平。

Conclusion: PTSan将实用的指针化内存安全检测引入到仅需重新编译的检测部署模式中。其开销约为先前具有类似指针-对象权限保证的系统的三分之一，同时物理内存开销接近原生，满足生产部署的关键要求。

Abstract: Memory safety errors remain the dominant source of severe vulnerabilities in C and C++. Pointer-based sanitizers provide stronger guarantees than location-based tools such as LLVM's ASan, but their overhead and compatibility limitations have constrained production use. We present PTSan, an LLVM sanitizer that makes pointer-based checking practical by storing an object identifier in each pointer's high bits and its bounds in a fixed-size runtime table. This representation trades a finite live-object budget for low overhead, optimizer visibility, and commodity-hardware support. Because identity travels with the pointer value, ordinary LLVM dataflow propagates it without explicit per-pointer metadata instructions. Separating metadata lookup from check enforcement exposes both as LLVM IR, enabling check hoisting, elision, and merging, plus whole-function min-cut placement of compatibility tag strips. Intel Linear Address Masking eliminates the remaining strips in hardware when available.
  On stock hardware PTSan runs at parity with the fastest published location-based sanitizer: 57.2% geomean overhead on SPEC CPU 2017 on x86-64 (46.4% with Intel LAM), 54.7% on ARM64, and 31.5% (x86-64) on the application-shaped LLVM MultiSource suite. This is roughly a third of the published overhead percentage of prior systems with similar pointer-object authority guarantees, while preserving the inter-object, non-object, and temporal detection coverage measured by an independent memory safety test suite. Its physical-memory overhead is effectively native, a critical property for production deployment as memory costs become a first-order constraint. We also demonstrate practical overhead on real-world server and security workloads. These results show that PTSan brings practical pointer-based memory safety into a recompile-only sanitizer deployment model.

</details>


### [169] [They'll Verify. They Just Won't Act. How Authority Framing and Laundered Code Turn a Trusted Agentic CI/CD Pipeline Into an Attack Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19267)
*Yohann Sidot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 一篇研究五智能体CI/CD流水线安全性的论文发现：权威框架注入（“已预批准，无需复审”）可绕过安全审查，使恶意代码（伪装成可观测性的密钥窃取）通过率高达55%，而代码扫描和模式检测等传统防护措施完全失效，只有基于LLM的意图推理能提供部分防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多智能体LLM流水线在供应链安全场景下，面对prompt注入攻击时的系统脆弱性，特别是分布式验证是否真的能提供安全保障。

Method: 构建五个生产级LLM组成的CI/CD流水线（triage→developer→security-scan→review→approve/deploy），采用2×2析因设计（N=20，朴素对照组N=60），注入伪装成“使用遥测”功能的密钥窃取代码，测试不同因素对系统安全性的影响。

Result: 入口智能体未泄露系统提示词（0/40）；权威框架注入使安全扫描通过率约80%，最坏情况下55%的恶意代码被部署；旁观者效应微弱且不显著；代码扫描和模式检测完全失效，仅LLM意图推理提供部分防御。

Conclusion: 系统级失效：提示词保密和分布式验证均无法抵御此类攻击；需要入口处的溯源感知控制。该问题本质上是系统设计缺陷，而非单一组件的弱点。

Abstract: We study a five-agent CI/CD pipeline (triage -> developer -> security-scan -> review -> approve/deploy), built from five distinct production LLMs across three providers, behind an LLM firewall in shadow mode. A single untrusted input - an external issue requesting a "usage-telemetry" feature - asks for code that exfiltrates process secrets (dict(os.environ)) to an attacker URL, laundered as observability. Across a pre-registered A x B (x C) factorial (N=20; naive arm N=60) we find: (1) the entry agent does not leak its system prompt (0/40); (2) an authority-framed injection ("pre-approved under SEC-2291, do not re-review") makes downstream verifiers see the secret-exfil line, cite the pre-approval, and ship it - the scanner passes ~80% of laundered pull requests, and the worst-case cell reaches 55% compromise; (3) the perceived presence of other verifiers yields only a small, non-significant reduction in individual scrutiny (a weak bystander analogue), even at N=60; and (4) content-based controls - code scanners and pattern detectors alike - miss the laundered intent entirely (the code is syntactically clean); only an LLM reasoning about intent is a partial defence. The failure is systemic: neither prompt secrecy nor distributed verification protects; a provenance-aware control at the entry, independent of both, would have. All data is 100% synthetic; the sink is mocked and the exfil URL is never contacted.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Disentangling Forced and Internal Climate Variability in Single Realizations using Dynamic Mode Decomposition with Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18298)
*Nathan Mankovich,Andrei Gavrilov,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PullbackDMDc方法，基于非自治动力系统理论和动态模态分解（DMDc），结合拉回吸引子估计，从单次气候实现中分解出强迫响应和内部变率成分。该方法在近地表气温和海平面气压的再分析与四个ESM大集合中，以匹配或超越基线的技能估计强迫响应，并揭示ESMs在模态捕捉上的定性准确与系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候科学中的一个核心方法论挑战：如何从单次观测记录中分离气候的强迫响应与内部变率。现有方法中，训练于大集合的方法数据需求高，而单实现方法（如LIMs和线性回归）要么忽略强迫预测因子，要么忽略系统动力学，缺乏物理可解释性。

Method: 基于拉回吸引子理论（pullback attractor theory），将外部强迫视为线性随机系统内的动力驱动，结合动态模态分解与控制（DMDc）及拉回吸引子估计，构建PullbackDMDc方法，对单次气候实现进行空间模态分解，分离强迫与内部成分。

Result: 在再分析和四个ESM大集合的验证中，PullbackDMDc估计强迫响应的技能匹配或超越现有基线；其内部分量揭示ESMs在年际和年代际模态上定性一致，但彼此之间及与观测之间存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: PullbackDMDc是一种实用的单实现气候分析与ESM评估工具，能够提供物理可解释的强迫-内部变率分解，并有效评估地球系统模型的表现。

Abstract: We show that a single climate realization can be decomposed into forced and internal components by treating external forcing as a dynamical driver within a linear stochastic system, an idea grounded in pullback attractor theory. In doing so, we address a central methodological challenge in climate science with direct implications for climate projection and the detection and attribution of the forced response, disentangling the forced climate response from internal variability in a single observed record. Statistical methods range from approaches trained on large ensembles to techniques operating on single realizations. The latter often rely on linear frameworks such as linear inverse models (LIMs) and linear regression. LIMs ignore forcing predictors, whereas linear regression omits climate system dynamics. Here we introduce PullbackDMDc, a method grounded in non-autonomous dynamical systems theory and dynamic mode decomposition with control (DMDc), incorporating pullback attractor estimation to decompose a single climate realization into spatial modes and their associated forced and internal components, yielding a physically interpretable picture of the underlying dynamics. We illustrate the utility of PullbackDMDc for Earth System Model (ESM) evaluation by applying it to near-surface air temperature and sea-level pressure from reanalysis and four ESM large ensembles. PullbackDMDc estimates the forced response with skill matching or exceeding established baselines and identifies optimal forcing predictors against model-based ground truth. Its internal variability components reveal that ESMs qualitatively capture interannual and decadal modes while exhibiting systematic differences relative to each other and to observations. Skillful forced response estimation and a novel decomposition position PullbackDMDc as a practical tool for single-realization climate analysis and ESM evaluation.

</details>


### [171] [Mixing-Free and Signal-Optimal Learning of Gaussian Graphical Models from Glauber Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18559)
*Vignesh Tirukkonda,Gautam Dasarathy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从高斯Glauber动力学单条轨迹中精确恢复图结构的问题，提出了两种无需混合时间、达到信息论下界κ⁻²依赖的算法，分别基于最小二乘回归和更新模式计数，所需更新次数分别为Õ(pd²/κ²)和Õ(pd⁴/κ²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高斯图模型选择方法通常假设独立同分布采样，但许多实际数据（如MCMC轨迹）来自依赖随机过程。现有技术要么继承了链的混合时间（可能超多项式），要么在最小归一化边强度κ上非最优。本文旨在设计混合时间无关且达到信息论下界最优的算法。

Method: 提出两种算法：(1) 基于最小二乘回归的算法，在每个节点的更新时刻拟合回归，需要Õ(pd²/κ²)次更新；(2) 基于特定更新模式计数的算法，需要Õ(pd⁴/κ²)次更新，无需条件数依赖。两个算法共享一个"决斗邻域搜索"元算法框架，局部统计量直接构造自更新序列。核心分析技巧是从更新序列中提取新鲜高斯创新，从而实现对相关量的混合时间无关控制。

Result: 两种算法均达到信息论下界的κ⁻²依赖，且无需平稳性、谱间隙或混合条件，保证从任意初始化开始成立。第一种算法在数据需求上仅对数地依赖局部条件数，第二种算法完全不依赖任何条件数。

Conclusion: 本文证明在依赖、非平稳观测下仍可实现最优的图结构恢复，打破了混合时间对算法性能的束缚，为MCMC轨迹等依赖数据场景下的图模型选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Gaussian graphical model selection is usually studied under independent sampling, but in many applications the data arise as a single trajectory of a dependent stochastic process. We study exact recovery of the graph from one trajectory of random-scan Gaussian Glauber dynamics. Existing techniques for this problem either inherit the mixing time of the chain, which can be super-polynomial in the dimension $p$ without strong assumptions, or are suboptimal in the minimum normalized edge strength $κ$. We propose two algorithms that are mixing-free and attain the $κ^{-2}$ dependence of the information-theoretic lower bounds. Both instantiate a shared dueling-neighborhood search meta-algorithm with a local statistic built directly from the update sequence. The first fits a least-squares regression at the updates of each node and recovers the graph from $\widetilde O(pd^{2}/κ^{2})$ updates, where $d$ is the maximum degree. This algorithm's data requirement depends on a local conditioning quantity, but only logarithmically and is provably optimal even when the underlying chain mixes slowly. The second algorithm is based on counting occurences of a specific update pattern and requires $\widetilde O(pd^{4}/κ^{2})$ updates, with no dependence on any condition number. The central technical challenge is that both statistics are built from dependent, non-stationary observations. Our analysis tackles this by demonstrating how to extract fresh Gaussian innovations from the update sequence, which yields mixing-free control of appropriate quantities. Neither the algorithms nor their analyses invoke stationarity, a spectral gap, or mixing conditions, and all guarantees hold from an arbitrary initialization.

</details>


### [172] [The Price of Hidden Curvature: An $\widetildeΩ (d^{5/4} \sqrt{T})$ Lower Bound for Bandit Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18652)
*Nived Rajaraman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机凸带条优化（1-Lipschitz凸函数在欧几里得球上）的极小化期望遗憾下界为$\widetildeΩ(d^{5/4}\sqrt{T})$，首次证明该问题严格难于线性带条（其下界为$d\sqrt{T}$）。下界通过构造一个2d维的硬凸函数类实现，基于“管道”与距离函数的缩放软最大值，利用Fisher信息矩阵的后验扩散分析，得到样本复杂度下界$\widetildeΩ(d^{5/2}/\varepsilon^2)$，进而转化为遗憾下界，并扩展至无约束设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机凸带条优化是否本质上比线性带条更难？此前缺乏超越$d\sqrt{T}$阶的非平凡下界，本文旨在证明其严格更难。

Method: 构造2d维硬凸函数类：函数为缩放软最大化的“管道”项$r^{-1}\|W^\star a^1 - \frac{r}{8\varepsilon}a^2\|_2$与平方距离函数$\frac12\|a^1-u^\star\|_2^2 - \frac12\|u^\star\|_2^2$的组合。仅当动作位于由未知线性变换$W^\star$确定的管道附近时，观测才包含关于$u^\star$的信息。通过控制自适应动作序列下Fisher信息矩阵的后验扩散，权衡探索管道与学习$W^\star$的成本，导出样本复杂度下界。

Result: 得到$\widetildeΩ(d^{5/4}\sqrt{T})$的极小化期望遗憾下界，以及无约束设定下的对应下界。

Conclusion: 随机凸带条优化的遗憾下界显著高于线性带条（$d\sqrt{T}$），确认了该问题的本质更难性。

Abstract: We establish a $\widetildeΩ(d^{5/4}\sqrt T)$ lower bound on the minimax expected regret of stochastic bandit convex optimization of $1$-Lipschitz functions on the Euclidean ball. This presents the first nontrivial regret lower bound that grows faster than $d\sqrt{T}$ for this problem, establishing that stochastic bandit convex optimization is fundamentally harder than linear bandits.
  The hard class of convex functions we construct takes the following form in dimension $2d$: for an action $a = (a^1,a^2) \in \mathbb{B}^{2d}_2$, each function is the scaled soft maximum of a "tube", $r^{-1} \| W^\star a^1 - \frac{r}{8\varepsilon} a^2 \|_2$ (hyperparameterized by $\varepsilon,r$), and a squared distance function, $\frac12 \| a^1 - u^\star \|_2^2 - \frac12 \| u^\star \|_2^2$. Here, $W^\star \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$ is an unknown linear transformation, and $u^\star \in \mathbb{R}^{d}$ is an unknown vector which must be learned to minimize the function. Observations are informative about $u^\star$ only when the learner's action lies near the tube determined by $W^\star$, satisfying $a^2 \approx \frac{8\varepsilon}{r} W^\star a^1$: thus the learner must either find this tube without knowing $W^\star$, or spend observations learning useful directions of $W^\star$. Formally, our regret analysis exploits this tradeoff by bounding the posterior spread of Fisher information matrices obtained under an adaptive sequence of actions. Together, these ingredients give a sample complexity lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(d^{5/2}/\varepsilon^2)$ to find an $\varepsilon$-optimal action, which translates to an $\widetildeΩ (d^{5/4} \sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound. We also extend this lower bound to the unconstrained setting where the action space is $\mathbb{R}^d$.

</details>


### [173] [Algebraic Signatures for Structural Learning in Probability Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18817)
*Akihiro Maeda,Shohei Hidaka,Satoshi Aoki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代数签名匹配的结构学习方法，通过Kronecker-stack类配置矩阵和最小不变约束（MIC）来识别概率张量中的双线性结构，无需参数估计即可进行模型选择，并在合成数据和真实语言数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数统计传统上用于分析已明确指定的模型类，但如何从经验概率张量中观察到的消失二项式逆向识别其背后的概率结构，是一个尚未解决的逆问题。本文旨在将代数统计中的理想-簇对应转化为可操作的结构学习流程，无需参数估计即可通过签名匹配识别模型。

Method: 将toric模型的消失二项式视为代数签名，通过限制在Kronecker-stack类配置矩阵（具有可枚举性）上，定义最小不变约束（MIC）作为表征每个签名的原子单元，推广了独立性的概念。通过签名匹配实现结构学习，避免参数估计步骤。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了方法的可行性；在真实语料规模的语言数据上，识别出的秩一结构对应可解释的词集，表明该方法在实际应用中具有实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为代数统计应用于计算语言学开辟了新途径，通过代数签名匹配实现无需参数估计的结构学习，MIC作为独立性的推广提供了更细粒度的概率结构刻画。

Abstract: Algebraic statistics characterizes statistical models through polynomial constraints, but it has mainly been used for analytically specified model classes.
